=== SOURCE CODE (py) ===
'''
# This is a class to simulate a vending machine, including adding products, inserting coins, purchasing products, viewing balance, replenishing product inventory, and displaying product information.

class VendingMachine:
    def __init__(self):
        """
        Initializes the vending machine's inventory and balance.
        """
        self.inventory = {}
        self.balance = 0

    def add_item(self, item_name, price, quantity):
        """
        Adds a product to the vending machine's inventory.
        :param item_name: The name of the product to be added, str.
        :param price: The price of the product to be added, float.
        :param quantity: The quantity of the product to be added, int.
        :return: None
        >>> vendingMachine = VendingMachine()
        >>> vendingMachine.add_item('Coke', 1.25, 10)
        >>> vendingMachine.inventory
        {'Coke': {'price': 1.25, 'quantity': 10}}

        """

    def insert_coin(self, amount):
        """
        Inserts coins into the vending machine.
        :param amount: The amount of coins to be inserted, float.
        :return: The balance of the vending machine after the coins are inserted, float.
        >>> vendingMachine = VendingMachine()
        >>> vendingMachine.insert_coin(1.25)
        1.25

        """

    def purchase_item(self, item_name):
        """
        Purchases a product from the vending machine and returns the balance after the purchase and display purchase unsuccessful if the product is out of stock.
        :param item_name: The name of the product to be purchased, str.
        :return: If successful, returns the balance of the vending machine after the product is purchased, float,otherwise,returns False.
        >>> vendingMachine = VendingMachine()
        >>> vendingMachine.inventory = {'Coke': {'price': 1.25, 'quantity': 10}}
        >>> vendingMachine.balance = 1.25
        >>> vendingMachine.purchase_item('Coke')
        0.0
        >>> vendingMachine.purchase_item('Pizza')
        False

        """

    def restock_item(self, item_name, quantity):
        """
        Replenishes the inventory of a product already in the vending machine.
        :param item_name: The name of the product to be replenished, str.
        :param quantity: The quantity of the product to be replenished, int.
        :return: If the product is already in the vending machine, returns True, otherwise, returns False.
        >>> vendingMachine = VendingMachine()
        >>> vendingMachine.inventory = {'Coke': {'price': 1.25, 'quantity': 10}}
        >>> vendingMachine.restock_item('Coke', 10)
        True
        >>> vendingMachine.restock_item('Pizza', 10)
        False

        """

    def display_items(self):
        """
        Displays the products in the vending machine.
        :return: If the vending machine is empty, returns False, otherwise, returns a list of the products in the vending machine, str.
        >>> vendingMachine = VendingMachine()
        >>> vendingMachine.display_items()
        False
        >>> vendingMachine.inventory = {'Coke': {'price': 1.25, 'quantity': 10} }
        >>> vendingMachine.display_items()
        'Coke - $1.25 [10]'

        """
'''

class VendingMachine:
    def __init__(self):
        self.inventory = {}
        self.balance = 0

    def add_item(self, item_name, price, quantity):
        if not self.restock_item(item_name, quantity):
            self.inventory[item_name] = {'price': price, 'quantity': quantity}

    def insert_coin(self, amount):
        self.balance += amount
        return self.balance

    def purchase_item(self, item_name):
        if item_name in self.inventory:
            item = self.inventory[item_name]
            if item['quantity'] > 0 and self.balance >= item['price']:
                self.balance -= item['price']
                item['quantity'] -= 1
                return self.balance
            else:
                return False
        else:
            return False

    def restock_item(self, item_name, quantity):
        if item_name in self.inventory:
            self.inventory[item_name]['quantity'] += quantity
            return True
        else:
            return False

    def display_items(self):
        if not self.inventory:
            return False
        else:
            items = []
            for item_name, item_info in self.inventory.items():
                items.append(f"{item_name} - ${item_info['price']} [{item_info['quantity']}]")
            return "\n".join(items)

```py
'''
# This is a class to simulate a vending machine, including adding products, inserting coins, purchasing products, viewing balance, replenishing product inventory, and displaying product information.

class VendingMachine:
    def __init__(self):
        """
        Initializes the vending machine's inventory and balance.
        """
        self.inventory = {}
        self.balance = 0

    def add_item(self, item_name, price, quantity):
        """
        Adds a product to the vending machine's inventory.
        :param item_name: The name of the product to be added, str.
        :param price: The price of the product to be added, float.
        :param quantity: The quantity of the product to be added, int.
        :return: None
        >>> vendingMachine = VendingMachine()
        >>> vendingMachine.add_item('Coke', 1.25, 10)
        >>> vendingMachine.inventory
        {'Coke': {'price': 1.25, 'quantity': 10}}

        """

    def insert_coin(self, amount):
        """
        Inserts coins into the vending machine.
        :param amount: The amount of coins to be inserted, float.
        :return: The balance of the vending machine after the coins are inserted, float.
        >>> vendingMachine = VendingMachine()
        >>> vendingMachine.insert_coin(1.25)
        1.25

        """

    def purchase_item(self, item_name):
        """
        Purchases a product from the vending machine and returns the balance after the purchase and display purchase unsuccessful if the product is out of stock.
        :param item_name: The name of the product to be purchased, str.
        :return: If successful, returns the balance of the vending machine after the product is purchased, float,otherwise,returns False.
        >>> vendingMachine = VendingMachine()
        >>> vendingMachine.inventory = {'Coke': {'price': 1.25, 'quantity': 10}}
        >>> vendingMachine.balance = 1.25
        >>> vendingMachine.purchase_item('Coke')
        0.0
        >>> vendingMachine.purchase_item('Pizza')
        False

        """

    def restock_item(self, item_name, quantity):
        """
        Replenishes the inventory of a product already in the vending machine.
        :param item_name: The name of the product to be replenished, str.
        :param quantity: The quantity of the product to be replenished, int.
        :return: If the product is already in the vending machine, returns True, otherwise, returns False.
        >>> vendingMachine = VendingMachine()
        >>> vendingMachine.inventory = {'Coke': {'price': 1.25, 'quantity': 10}}
        >>> vendingMachine.restock_item('Coke', 10)
        True
        >>> vendingMachine.restock_item('Pizza', 10)
        False

        """

    def display_items(self):
        """
        Displays the products in the vending machine.
        :return: If the vending machine is empty, returns False, otherwise, returns a list of the products in the vending machine, str.
        >>> vendingMachine = VendingMachine()
        >>> vendingMachine.display_items()
        False
        >>> vendingMachine.inventory = {'Coke': {'price': 1.25, 'quantity': 10} }
        >>> vendingMachine.display_items()
        'Coke - $1.25 [10]'

        """
'''

class VendingMachine:
    def __init__(self):
        self.inventory = {}
        self.balance = 0

    def add_item(self, item_name, price, quantity):
        if not self.restock_item(item_name, quantity):
            self.inventory[item_name] = {'price': price, 'quantity': quantity}

    def insert_coin(self, amount):
        self.balance += amount
        return self.balance

    def purchase_item(self, item_name):
        if item_name in self.inventory:
            item = self.inventory[item_name]
            if item['quantity'] > 0 and self.balance >= item['price']:
                self.balance -= item['price']
                item['quantity'] -= 1
                return self.balance
            else:
                return False
        else:
            return False

    def restock_item(self, item_name, quantity):
        if item_name in self.inventory:
            self.inventory[item_name]['quantity'] += quantity
            return True
        else:
            return False

    def display_items(self):
        if not self.inventory:
            return False
        else:
            items = []
            for item_name, item_info in self.inventory.items():
                items.append(f"{item_name} - ${item_info['price']} [{item_info['quantity']}]")
            return "\n".join(items)
```

Let's write a helper:

std::string format_double(double value) {
    if (value == 0) return "0.0"; // Python repr(0.0) is '0.0'
    // Handle negative
    bool neg = std::signbit(value);
    if (neg) value = -value;
    char buf[128];
    auto res = std::to_chars(buf, buf + sizeof(buf), value, std::chars_format::scientific);
    std::string sci(buf, res.ptr); // e.g. "1.25e+00" or "1e+15" or "1.5e-10"
    // Parse sci: mantissa, exponent
    // Find 'e'
    size_t epos = sci.find('e');
    std::string mant = sci.substr(0, epos);
    int exp = std::stoi(sci.substr(epos+1));
    // Remove decimal point from mantissa to get digits
    std::string digits;
    for (char c : mant) if (c != '.') digits += c;
    // Determine exponent of the first digit: if mantissa is "d.ddd", the value is 0.digits * 10^(exp+1)? Actually "1.25e+03" = 1.25 * 10^3 = 1250. The digits "125" with decimal point after first digit. The value's decimal exponent (position of decimal point relative to digits) is exp + 1? Let's define: digits = "125", exponent e = exp+1? For 1.25e+03, digits "125", value = 0.125 * 10^4? Wait 1.25e3 = 1250. digits "125", if we place decimal after first digit: 1.25 * 10^3. So the exponent of the first digit is 3. In general, if mantissa is "d.ddd", the first digit's power of 10 is exp. So the number of digits before decimal point is exp+1 if exp >= 0. If exp < 0, decimal point is before digits.
    // Python formatting rules:
    // Let exponent = exp (power of first digit). Let digits = string of significant digits (no leading zeros? to_chars scientific gives normalized mantissa so first digit nonzero).
    // If -4 <= exponent < 16, use decimal notation.
    // Else use scientific notation: first digit, then '.' if more digits, then rest, then 'e', then sign, then exponent (no leading zeros? Python uses e+20, e-10, e+05? Actually Python repr(1e-5) = '1e-05'? Let's check: Python repr(1e-5) -> '1e-05'. It has two digits for exponent? I think Python uses at least two digits? Let's recall: repr(1e-5) is '1e-05'. repr(1e20) is '1e+20'. repr(1e15) is '1000000000000000.0'. repr(1e16) is '1e+16'. So exponent has at least two digits? For 1e20, '1e+20' has two digits. For 1e-5, '1e-05' has two digits. For 1e100, '1e+100' has three. So it uses at least two digits, with leading zero if needed. Yes.
    // Decimal notation:
    // If exponent >= 0: place decimal point after exponent+1 digits. If digits length <= exponent+1, append zeros. If exactly, append ".0". If more, insert dot.
    // If exponent < 0: "0." + zeros(-exponent-1) + digits
    // But note: Python repr for 0.0 is "0.0". For values like 0.0001 (1e-4) exponent = -4, which is >= -4, so decimal notation "0.0001". For 1e-5 exponent=-5, scientific "1e-05".
    // Need to handle trailing zeros in digits? to_chars scientific gives shortest digits, no trailing zeros.
}

Let's test with examples:
- 1.25: scientific "1.25e+00" -> digits "125", exp=0. exp >= -4 and < 16, decimal. digits length 3, exp+1=1. Need insert decimal after 1 digit: "1.25". Good.
- 1.0: scientific "1e+00" -> digits "1", exp=0. decimal. digits length 1, exp+1=1. No more digits, append ".0" -> "1.0". Good.
- 0.1: scientific "1e-01" -> digits "1", exp=-1. decimal because exp >= -4. exp < 0: "0." + zeros(-exp-1 = 0) + digits = "0.1". Good.
- 0.0001: scientific "1e-04" -> exp=-4, decimal. zeros(3) + "1" -> "0.0001". Good.
- 1e-5: scientific "1e-05" -> exp=-5, scientific notation: "1e-05". Good.
- 1e15: scientific "1e+15" -> exp=15, decimal (since <16). digits "1", exp+1=16, append 15 zeros? Wait digits length 1, need 16 digits before decimal? Actually value = 1 * 10^15 = 1000000000000000.0. In decimal notation, digits "1", exponent=15 means there are 15 zeros after the 1. So we need to append zeros until length = exp+1 = 16. So "1000000000000000" then append ".0" -> "1000000000000000.0". Good.
- 1e16: scientific "1e+16" -> exp=16, scientific notation: "1e+16". Good.
- 123.456: scientific "1.23456e+02" -> digits "123456", exp=2. decimal. exp+1=3, insert dot after 3 digits: "123.456". Good.

Let's implement `format_double` carefully.

Pseudo:

std::string format_double(double value) {
    bool neg = std::signbit(value);
    if (value == 0.0) {
        return neg ? "-0.0" : "0.0";
    }
    if (neg) value = -value;
    char buf[128];
    auto res = std::to_chars(buf, buf + sizeof(buf), value, std::chars_format::scientific);
    std::string sci(buf, res.ptr);
    // sci like "1.25e+00"
    size_t epos = sci.find('e');
    std::string mant = sci.substr(0, epos);
    int exp = std::stoi(sci.substr(epos + 1));
    // digits without dot
    std::string digits;
    for (char c : mant) if (c != '.') digits += c;
    // Remove leading zeros? to_chars scientific should not have leading zeros.
    // Now format
    std::string out;
    if (neg) out += '-';
    if (exp >= -4 && exp < 16) {
        // decimal notation
        if (exp >= 0) {
            int point_pos = exp + 1;
            if (digits.size() > point_pos) {
                out += digits.substr(0, point_pos) + "." + digits.substr(point_pos);
            } else {
                out += digits;
                out.append(point_pos - digits.size(), '0');
                out += ".0";
            }
        } else {
            out += "0.";
            out.append(-exp - 1, '0');
            out += digits;
        }
    } else {
        // scientific notation
        out += digits[0];
        if (digits.size() > 1) {
            out += ".";
            out += digits.substr(1);
        }
        out += 'e';
        if (exp >= 0) out += '+'; else out += '-';
        int abs_exp = std::abs(exp);
        // at least two digits
        if (abs_exp < 10) out += '0';
        out += std::to_string(abs_exp);
    }
    return out;
}

- 0.1: digits "1", exp -1. decimal exp<0 -> "0." + zeros(0) + "1" = "0.1". Good.

Now, define `using PurchaseResult = std::variant<double, bool>;` and `using DisplayResult = std::variant<bool, std::string>;`.

Class:

class VendingMachine {
private:
    struct Item {
        double price;
        int quantity;
    };
    std::unordered_map<std::string, Item> inventory;
    std::vector<std::string> order;
    double balance;

    static std::string format_double(double value) { ... }

public:
    VendingMachine() : balance(0.0) {}

    void add_item(const std::string& item_name, double price, int quantity) {
        if (!restock_item(item_name, quantity)) {
            inventory[item_name] = {price, quantity};
            order.push_back(item_name);
        }
    }

    double insert_coin(double amount) {
        balance += amount;
        return balance;
    }

    PurchaseResult purchase_item(const std::string& item_name) {
        auto it = inventory.find(item_name);
        if (it != inventory.end()) {
            Item& item = it->second;
            if (item.quantity > 0 && balance >= item.price) {
                balance -= item.price;
                item.quantity -= 1;
                return balance;
            } else {
                return false;
            }
        } else {
            return false;
        }
    }

    bool restock_item(const std::string& item_name, int quantity) {
        auto it = inventory.find(item_name);
        if (it != inventory.end()) {
            it->second.quantity += quantity;
            return true;
        } else {
            return false;
        }
    }

    DisplayResult display_items() {
        if (inventory.empty()) {
            return false;
        } else {
            std::string result;
            for (size_t i = 0; i < order.size(); ++i) {
                const std::string& name = order[i];
                const Item& item = inventory.at(name);
                if (i > 0) result += "\n";
                result += name + " - $" + format_double(item.price) + " [" + std::to_string(item.quantity) + "]";
            }
            return result;
        }
    }
};

Need to ensure `inventory.at(name)` works; if order has name not in inventory, would throw. Not happen.

Now, format_double implementation:

static std::string format_double(double value) {
    if (std::isnan(value)) return "nan";
    if (std::isinf(value)) return std::signbit(value) ? "-inf" : "inf";
    bool neg = std::signbit(value);
    if (value == 0.0) {
        return neg ? "-0.0" : "0.0";
    }
    if (neg) value = -value;
    char buf[128];
    auto res = std::to_chars(buf, buf + sizeof(buf), value, std::chars_format::scientific);
    std::string sci(buf, res.ptr);
    size_t epos = sci.find('e');
    std::string mant = sci.substr(0, epos);
    int exp = std::stoi(sci.substr(epos + 1));
    std::string digits;
    for (char c : mant) {
        if (c != '.') digits += c;
    }
    std::string out;
    if (neg) out += '-';
    if (exp >= -4 && exp < 16) {
        if (exp >= 0) {
            int point_pos = exp + 1;
            if (digits.size() > static_cast<size_t>(point_pos)) {
                out += digits.substr(0, point_pos);
                out += ".";
                out += digits.substr(point_pos);
            } else {
                out += digits;
                out.append(point_pos - digits.size(), '0');
                out += ".0";
            }
        } else {
            out += "0.";
            out.append(-exp - 1, '0');
            out += digits;
        }
    } else {
        out += digits[0];
        if (digits.size() > 1) {
            out += ".";
            out += digits.substr(1);
        }
        out += 'e';
        if (exp >= 0) out += '+'; else out += '-';
        int abs_exp = std::abs(exp);
        if (abs_exp < 10) out += '0';
        out += std::to_string(abs_exp);
    }
    return out;
}

Need to include <cstddef>? size_t from <string> etc.

Potential issue: `point_pos - digits.size()` where point_pos is int, digits.size() is size_t. We cast? In C++, `point_pos - digits.size()` will convert point_pos to size_t, could underflow if point_pos < digits.size()? But we are in else branch where digits.size() <= point_pos, so point_pos - digits.size() is non-negative. But because point_pos is int and digits.size() is size_t, the result is size_t. `out.append(point_pos - digits.size(), '0')` expects size_t. Fine. But if point_pos is int and digits.size() is size_t, the subtraction is done in unsigned, but since digits.size() <= point_pos, it's safe. However, to avoid warnings, cast: `out.append(static_cast<size_t>(point_pos) - digits.size(), '0');`. Let's do that.

Similarly, `out.append(-exp - 1, '0')` where -exp-1 is int. append expects size_t. It converts. Fine.

Potential issue: `digits[0]` if digits empty? Not possible because scientific notation always has at least one digit. Good.

Potential issue: `std::to_chars` for value like 1.0 might produce "1e+00" and digits "1". Good.

For 1e15, exponent=15, precision=15, `%g` uses decimal? `%g` uses scientific if exponent >= precision, so 15 >= 15, so scientific "1e+15". But Python uses decimal for 1e15. So mismatch. So precision 15 not good.

Could use `std::setprecision(16)`? For 1e15, exponent=15, precision=16, decimal "1000000000000000". Append ".0" -> correct. For 1e16, exponent=16, precision=16, scientific "1e+16" -> correct. For 0.1, with precision 16, output "0.1" (since 0.1 has 16 significant digits? Actually 0.1 = 0.1000000000000000, with 16 significant digits it's "0.1" because trailing zeros omitted). For 1.25, "1.25". For 1.0, "1". Append ".0". For 1.2345678901234567, with precision 16, "1.234567890123457" (rounded) not exact. Python repr would be "1.2345678901234567". So precision 16 loses digits. Need 17 for round-trip. But 17 causes 0.1 issue. So to_chars is necessary.

Thus, we'll use to_chars.

Now, let's consider if `std::to_chars` for 0.1 with scientific produces "1e-01". Then our format gives "0.1". Good. So to_chars solves.

Now, let's finalize.

One more thing: In `format_double`, we use `std::stoi` which may throw if exponent is empty. Not.

Now, let's write the code with `#include <charconv>`.

Let's also consider if `std::to_chars` for `double` requires `std::chars_format` and no precision; yes.

Now, let's produce final answer.

But wait, the instruction says "Use a short stable mapping path that preserves behavior." and "Keep the reasoning compact and single-pass" but we don't need to output reasoning, only code. So final code block.

Let's write it cleanly.

```cpp
#include <string>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <vector>
#include <variant>
#include <charconv>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdlib>

class VendingMachine {
private:
    struct Item {
        double price;
        int quantity;
    };

    std::unordered_map<std::string, Item> inventory;
    std::vector<std::string> order;
    double balance;

    static std::string format_double(double value) {
        if (std::isnan(value)) return "nan";
        if (std::isinf(value)) return std::signbit(value) ? "-inf" : "inf";

        bool neg = std::signbit(value);
        if (value == 0.0) {
            return neg ? "-0.0" : "0.0";
        }
        if (neg) value = -value;

        char buf[128];
        auto res = std::to_chars(buf, buf + sizeof(buf), value, std::chars_format::scientific);
        std::string sci(buf, res.ptr);

        size_t epos = sci.find('e');
        std::string mant = sci.substr(0, epos);
        int exp = std::stoi(sci.substr(epos + 1));

        std::string digits;
        for (char c : mant) {
            if (c != '.') digits += c;
        }
        while (digits.size() > 1 && digits.back() == '0') {
            digits.pop_back();
        }

        std::string out;
        if (neg) out += '-';

        if (exp >= -4 && exp < 16) {
            if (exp >= 0) {
                int point_pos = exp + 1;
                if (digits.size() > static_cast<size_t>(point_pos)) {
                    out += digits.substr(0, point_pos);
                    out += ".";
                    out += digits.substr(point_pos);
                } else {
                    out += digits;
                    out.append(static_cast<size_t>(point_pos) - digits.size(), '0');
                    out += ".0";
                }
            } else {
                out += "0.";
                out.append(static_cast<size_t>(-exp - 1), '0');
                out += digits;
            }
        } else {
            out += digits[0];
            if (digits.size() > 1) {
                out += ".";
                out += digits.substr(1);
            }
            out += 'e';
            if (exp >= 0) out += '+';
            else out += '-';
            int abs_exp = std::abs(exp);
            if (abs_exp < 10) out += '0';
            out += std::to_string(abs_exp);
        }
        return out;
    }

public:
    VendingMachine() : balance(0.0) {}

    void add_item(const std::string& item_name, double price, int quantity) {
        if (!restock_item(item_name, quantity)) {
            inventory[item_name] = {price, quantity};
            order.push_back(item_name);
        }
    }

    double insert_coin(double amount) {
        balance += amount;
        return balance;
    }

    std::variant<double, bool> purchase_item(const std::string& item_name) {
        auto it = inventory.find(item_name);
        if (it != inventory.end()) {
            Item& item = it->second;
            if (item.quantity > 0 && balance >= item.price) {
                balance -= item.price;
                item.quantity -= 1;
                return balance;
            } else {
                return false;
            }
        } else {
            return false;
        }
    }

    bool restock_item(const std::string& item_name, int quantity) {
        auto it = inventory.find(item_name);
        if (it != inventory.end()) {
            it->second.quantity += quantity;
            return true;
        } else {
            return false;
        }
    }

    std::variant<bool, std::string> display_items() {
        if (inventory.empty()) {
            return false;
        } else {
            std::string result;
            for (size_t i = 0; i < order.size(); ++i) {
                if (i > 0) result += "\n";
                const std::string& name = order[i];
                const Item& item = inventory.at(name);
                result += name + " - $" + format_double(item.price) + " [" + std::to_string(item.quantity) + "]";
            }
            return result;
        }
    }
};
```

Potential issue: `std::signbit` is in <cmath>. Good.

Potential issue: `std::isnan`, `std::isinf` in <cmath>. Good.

Now, let's consider if `std::to_chars` scientific for 
</think>

=== RAW ANSWER ===
```cpp
#include <string>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <vector>
#include <variant>
#include <charconv>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdlib>

class VendingMachine {
private:
    struct Item {
        double price;
        int quantity;
    };

    std::unordered_map<std::string, Item> inventory;
    std::vector<std::string> order;
    double balance;

    static std::string format_double(double value) {
        if (std::isnan(value)) return "nan";
        if (std::isinf(value)) return std::signbit(value) ? "-inf" : "inf";

        bool neg = std::signbit(value);
        if (value == 0.0) {
            return neg ? "-0.0" : "0.0";
        }
        if (neg) value = -value;

        char buf[128];
        auto res = std::to_chars(buf, buf + sizeof(buf), value, std::chars_format::scientific);
        std::string sci(buf, res.ptr);

        size_t epos = sci.find('e');
        std::string mant = sci.substr(0, epos);
        int exp = std::stoi(sci.substr(epos + 1));

        std::string digits;
        for (char c : mant) {
            if (c != '.') digits += c;
        }
        while (digits.size() > 1 && digits.back() == '0') {
            digits.pop_back();
        }

        std::string out;
        if (neg) out += '-';

        if (exp >= -4 && exp < 16) {
            if (exp >= 0) {
                int point_pos = exp + 1;
                if (digits.size() > static_cast<size_t>(point_pos)) {
                    out += digits.substr(0, point_pos);
                    out += ".";
                    out += digits.substr(point_pos);
                } else {
                    out += digits;
                    out.append(static_cast<size_t>(point_pos) - digits.size(), '0');
                    out += ".0";
                }
            } else {
                out += "0.";
                out.append(static_cast<size_t>(-exp - 1), '0');
                out += digits;
            }
        } else {
            out += digits[0];
            if (digits.size() > 1) {
                out += ".";
                out += digits.substr(1);
            }
            out += 'e';
            if (exp >= 0) out += '+';
            else out += '-';
            int abs_exp = std::abs(exp);
            if (abs_exp < 10) out += '0';
            out += std::to_string(abs_exp);
        }
        return out;
    }

public:
    VendingMachine() : balance(0.0) {}

    void add_item(const std::string& item_name, double price, int quantity) {
        if (!restock_item(item_name, quantity)) {
            inventory[item_name] = {price, quantity};
            order.push_back(item_name);
        }
    }

    double insert_coin(double amount) {
        balance += amount;
        return balance;
    }

    std::variant<double, bool> purchase_item(const std::string& item_name) {
        auto it = inventory.find(item_name);
        if (it != inventory.end()) {
            Item& item = it->second;
            if (item.quantity > 0 && balance >= item.price) {
                balance -= item.price;
                item.quantity -= 1;
                return balance;
            } else {
                return false;
            }
        } else {
            return false;
        }
    }

    bool restock_item(const std::string& item_name, int quantity) {
        auto it = inventory.find(item_name);
        if (it != inventory.end()) {
            it->second.quantity += quantity;
            return true;
        } else {
            return false;
        }
    }

    std::variant<bool, std::string> display_items() {
        if (inventory.empty()) {
            return false;
        } else {
            std::string result;
            for (size_t i = 0; i < order.size(); ++i) {
                if (i > 0) result += "\n";
                const std::string& name = order[i];
                const Item& item = inventory.at(name);
                result += name + " - $" + format_double(item.price) + " [" + std::to_string(item.quantity) + "]";
            }
            return result;
        }
    }
};
```

=== TRANSLATED CODE (cpp) ===
#include <string>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <vector>
#include <variant>
#include <charconv>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdlib>

class VendingMachine {
private:
    struct Item {
        double price;
        int quantity;
    };

    std::unordered_map<std::string, Item> inventory;
    std::vector<std::string> order;
    double balance;

    static std::string format_double(double value) {
        if (std::isnan(value)) return "nan";
        if (std::isinf(value)) return std::signbit(value) ? "-inf" : "inf";

        bool neg = std::signbit(value);
        if (value == 0.0) {
            return neg ? "-0.0" : "0.0";
        }
        if (neg) value = -value;

        char buf[128];
        auto res = std::to_chars(buf, buf + sizeof(buf), value, std::chars_format::scientific);
        std::string sci(buf, res.ptr);

        size_t epos = sci.find('e');
        std::string mant = sci.substr(0, epos);
        int exp = std::stoi(sci.substr(epos + 1));

        std::string digits;
        for (char c : mant) {
            if (c != '.') digits += c;
        }
        while (digits.size() > 1 && digits.back() == '0') {
            digits.pop_back();
        }

        std::string out;
        if (neg) out += '-';

        if (exp >= -4 && exp < 16) {
            if (exp >= 0) {
                int point_pos = exp + 1;
                if (digits.size() > static_cast<size_t>(point_pos)) {
                    out += digits.substr(0, point_pos);
                    out += ".";
                    out += digits.substr(point_pos);
                } else {
                    out += digits;
                    out.append(static_cast<size_t>(point_pos) - digits.size(), '0');
                    out += ".0";
                }
            } else {
                out += "0.";
                out.append(static_cast<size_t>(-exp - 1), '0');
                out += digits;
            }
        } else {
            out += digits[0];
            if (digits.size() > 1) {
                out += ".";
                out += digits.substr(1);
            }
            out += 'e';
            if (exp >= 0) out += '+';
            else out += '-';
            int abs_exp = std::abs(exp);
            if (abs_exp < 10) out += '0';
            out += std::to_string(abs_exp);
        }
        return out;
    }

public:
    VendingMachine() : balance(0.0) {}

    void add_item(const std::string& item_name, double price, int quantity) {
        if (!restock_item(item_name, quantity)) {
            inventory[item_name] = {price, quantity};
            order.push_back(item_name);
        }
    }

    double insert_coin(double amount) {
        balance += amount;
        return balance;
    }

    std::variant<double, bool> purchase_item(const std::string& item_name) {
        auto it = inventory.find(item_name);
        if (it != inventory.end()) {
            Item& item = it->second;
            if (item.quantity > 0 && balance >= item.price) {
                balance -= item.price;
                item.quantity -= 1;
                return balance;
            } else {
                return false;
            }
        } else {
            return false;
        }
    }

    bool restock_item(const std::string& item_name, int quantity) {
        auto it = inventory.find(item_name);
        if (it != inventory.end()) {
            it->second.quantity += quantity;
            return true;
        } else {
            return false;
        }
    }

    std::variant<bool, std::string> display_items() {
        if (inventory.empty()) {
            return false;
        } else {
            std::string result;
            for (size_t i = 0; i < order.size(); ++i) {
                if (i > 0) result += "\n";
                const std::string& name = order[i];
                const Item& item = inventory.at(name);
                result += name + " - $" + format_double(item.price) + " [" + std::to_string(item.quantity) + "]";
            }
            return result;
        }
    }
};
